          private func handleReady(_ frame: Frame) {
              switch frame.kind {
              case .catalog:
                  guard let catalog = try? MessageCodec.decode(CaptureCatalog.self, from: frame.payload) else {
                      sessionLog.error("could not decode a capture catalog")
                      return
                  }
                  lock.withLock { _catalog = catalog }
                  emit(.catalog(catalog))
      
              case .pong:
                  reportLatency()
      
              case .notice:
                  // Decoded leniently on purpose. A notice is advisory — the session is healthy and
                  // this is only a caption for it — so a payload this build cannot read costs the
                  // caption and not the connection.
                  let payload = try? MessageCodec.decode(NoticePayload.self, from: frame.payload)
                  emit(.remoteNotice(payload))
      
              case .error:
                  let payload = try? MessageCodec.decode(ErrorPayload.self, from: frame.payload)
                  fail(payload?.message ?? "The Mac reported an error.")
      
              case .clipboardContent:
                  if let payload = try? MessageCodec.decode(ClipboardPayload.self, from: frame.payload) {
                      emit(.clipboard(payload.text))
                  }
      
              case .terminalEvent:
                  if let event = try? MessageCodec.decode(TerminalEvent.self, from: frame.payload) {
                      emit(.terminal(event))
                  }
      
              case .videoConfig, .videoFrame, .audioConfig, .audioFrame:
                  handleVideoFrame(frame)
      
              default:
                  sessionLog.notice("ignoring unexpected \(String(describing: frame.kind), privacy: .public) on a ready session")
              }
          }
      
          /// Sends a command, subject to the coalescing rules in ``CommandCoalescer``.
          ///
          /// This is the only path input takes. Nothing in the UI sends directly, so the rule that
          /// a 120 Hz touch stream becomes a 60 Hz command stream — and that a `mouseUp` is never
          /// dropped even when the queue is saturated — holds in one place instead of in each view.
          public func send(_ command: RemoteCommand) {
              guard isReady else { return }
              coalescer.enqueue(command)
          }
      
          /// Switches what the Mac is capturing. Sent as an ordinary command so it rides the same
          /// ordered channel as input, and marked critical so it survives a saturated queue.
          public func selectCaptureSource(_ source: CaptureSource) {
              send(.setCaptureSource(source))
          }
      
          /// Sends a terminal request directly on the authenticated control stream. Unlike pointer
          /// moves, terminal bytes must never be coalesced or reordered.
          public func sendTerminal(_ request: TerminalRequest) {
              guard isReady else { return }
              connection()?.send(.terminalRequest, request)
          }
      
          private func sendNow(_ command: RemoteCommand) {
              connection()?.send(command)
          }
      
          // MARK: - Liveness
      
          /// How often a control ping goes out.
          ///
          /// One second, where this was five. The figure is shown to someone who is watching it while
          /// they drag something, and a reading taken every five seconds describes a moment that has
          /// already passed by the time it appears — which is most of why it looked like it was
          /// jumping. A ping is one small frame and the control channel is otherwise idle, so the
          /// cost of asking five times as often is nothing.
          private static let pingInterval: Duration = .seconds(1)
      
      
          private func handleCommand(_ frame: Frame) throws {
              switch frame.kind {
              case .command:
                  guard frame.payload.count <= Self.maxControlPayload else {
                      throw SessionError.malformedPayload(.command, SessionError.protocolMismatch(client: 0, host: 0))
                  }
                  let command = try decode(RemoteCommand.self, from: frame, as: .command)
                  switch command {
                  case .requestClipboard:
                      if let text = onRequestClipboard?() {
                          send(MessageKind.clipboardContent, ClipboardPayload(text: text))
                      }
                  case .pushClipboard(let text):
                      onReceiveClipboard?(text)
                  default:
                      onCommand?(command)
                  }
      
              case .ping:
                  connection.send(Frame(kind: .pong))
      
              case .terminalRequest:
                  guard frame.payload.count <= Self.maxControlPayload else {
                      throw SessionError.malformedPayload(.terminalRequest, SessionError.protocolMismatch(client: 0, host: 0))
                  }
                  let request = try decode(TerminalRequest.self, from: frame, as: .terminalRequest)
                  onTerminalRequest?(request) { [weak self] event in
                      self?.sendTerminalEvent(event)
                  }
      
              default:
                  throw SessionError.unexpectedMessage(frame.kind)
              }
          }
      
          /// Proactively pushes updated clipboard content to the client without a request.
          func sendClipboard(_ text: String) {
              guard isAuthorized else { return }
              send(MessageKind.clipboardContent, ClipboardPayload(text: text))
          }
      
          func sendTerminalEvent(_ event: TerminalEvent) {
              guard isAuthorized else { return }
              send(.terminalEvent, event)
          }
      
          // MARK: - Helpers
      
          private func decode<T: Decodable>(_ type: T.Type, from frame: Frame, as kind: MessageKind) throws -> T {
              do {
                  return try MessageCodec.decode(type, from: frame.payload)
              } catch {
                  throw SessionError.malformedPayload(kind, error)
              }
          }
          private func accept(_ connection: NWConnection) {
              let relayBridge = pendingRelayBridges.isEmpty ? nil : pendingRelayBridges.removeFirst()
              if let relayBridge { activeRelayBridges.append(relayBridge) }
              let peer = PeerConnection(connection: connection, queue: queue)
      
              let session = HostSession(
                  connection: peer,
                  identity: identity,
                  device: device,
                  pins: pins,
                  emit: { [weak self] event in self?.emit(event) }
              )
              session.onCommand = { [weak self] command in
                  self?.onCommand?(command)
              }
              session.onRequestClipboard = { [weak self] in
                  self?.onRequestClipboard?()
              }
              session.onReceiveClipboard = { [weak self] text in
                  self?.onReceiveClipboard?(text)
              }
              session.onTerminalRequest = { [weak self, weak session] request, sink in
                  guard let self, let session else { return }
                  self.terminalManager.handle(clientID: session.clientID, request: request, sink: sink)
              }
              session.onVideoConnection = { [weak self] videoPeer in
                  guard let self else { return }
                  guard let key = videoPeer.peerPublicKey,
                        controlSessions.contains(where: { $0.isAuthorized && $0.peerPublicKey == key })
                  else {
                      // **This is the only thing authorizing a video connection, so it is not a
                      // formality.** A video connection carries no handshake of its own — it does
                      // not even say which client it belongs to — and the server's TLS verify
                      // block deliberately accepts any self-signed chain, because authenticity in
                      // this design comes from pairing rather than from a CA. So "completed a TLS
                      // handshake" proves nothing at all, and without this check any peer that can
                      // reach the port could ask for the user's screen and get it.
                      //
                      // The client key is the thing that ties the two sockets together: it comes
                      // from the peer's certificate, so a client cannot claim one it does not hold.
                      videoLog.notice("refused a video connection with no authorized control session")
                      emit(.failed("Refused a video connection from an unauthorized client"))
                      videoPeer.cancel()
                      return
                  }
      
                  videoConnections.append((peer: videoPeer, clientKey: key))
                  onVideoConnection?(videoPeer)
              }
              // Set for every session, not only the relayed ones, because it is now the moment the
              // advertisement is withdrawn as well as the moment a relayed video connection is handed
              // over — see ``updateAdvertisement()``.
              session.onAuthorized = { [weak self] controlPeer in
                  guard let self else { return }
                  self.updateAdvertisement()
                  // The remote transport multiplexes encoded video onto this same TLS stream. The
                  // relay still cannot read it; it is the same authenticated connection as control.
                  if relayBridge != nil {
                      self.onVideoConnection?(controlPeer)
                  }
              }
      
              session.onEnded = { [weak self] in
                  guard let self else { return }
                  self.terminalManager.disconnect(clientID: session.clientID)
                  // Pruning matters for more than tidiness — a finished session left in the list
                  // would go on authorizing video connections, since `isAuthorized` stays true on
                  // an object whose connection is gone.
                  controlSessions.removeAll { $0 === session }
                  // And the streams authorized by it end with it. "Stop sharing" has to actually
                  // stop: a video connection that outlived its control session would keep
                  // delivering the screen to a client the host believes is gone.
                  closeVideoConnections(for: session.peerPublicKey)
                  // Last one out puts the Mac back on the network.
                  self.updateAdvertisement()
                  if let relayBridge {
                      relayBridge.cancel()
                      activeRelayBridges.removeAll { $0 === relayBridge }
                  }
              }

      import RemoteCore
      import SwiftUI
      
      /// A deliberately small native terminal surface. The Mac owns the PTY and shell; this view only
      /// displays its bounded byte history and sends exact input bytes over the authenticated session.
      struct TerminalPanel: View {
          @Environment(SessionModel.self) private var model
          @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
          @State private var command = ""
          @State private var newName = ""
          @State private var showsCreate = false
          @FocusState private var inputFocused: Bool
      
          private var selected: RemoteTerminal? {
              guard let id = model.selectedTerminalID else { return nil }
              return model.terminals.first { $0.id == id }
          }
      
          var body: some View {
              NavigationStack {
                  VStack(spacing: 0) {
                      tabStrip
                      Divider().opacity(0.35)
                      if let terminal = selected {
                          output(terminal)
                          keyStrip(terminal.id)
                          input(terminal)
                      } else {
                          ContentUnavailableView(
                              "No terminals",
                              systemImage: "terminal",
                              description: Text("Create a login shell on your Mac. It keeps running if this phone disconnects.")
                          )
                      }
                  }
                  .background(Color.black)
                  .navigationTitle("Mac terminals")
                  .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
                  .toolbar {
                      ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                          Button("Done") { dismiss() }
                      }
                      ToolbarItemGroup(placement: .primaryAction) {
                          Button { model.refreshTerminals() } label: {
                              Image(systemName: "arrow.clockwise")
                          }
                          Button { showsCreate = true } label: {
                              Image(systemName: "plus")
                          }
                          .accessibilityLabel("New terminal")
                      }
                  }
              }
              .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
              .onAppear { model.refreshTerminals() }
              .alert("New terminal", isPresented: $showsCreate) {
                  TextField("Name, for example Build", text: $newName)
                  Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
                  Button("Create") {
                      model.createTerminal(name: newName)
                      newName = ""
                  }
              } message: {
                  Text("Starts your Mac login shell in your home folder.")
              }
          }
      
          private var tabStrip: some View {
              ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                  HStack(spacing: 8) {
                      ForEach(model.terminals) { terminal in
                          Button {
                              model.selectTerminal(terminal.id)
                          } label: {
                              HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                  Circle()
                                      .fill(terminal.isRunning ? Color.green : Color.secondary)
                                      .frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                                  Text(terminal.name).lineLimit(1)
                                  if terminal.attention != nil {
                                      Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.circle.fill")
                                          .foregroundStyle(.orange)
                                  }
                              }
                              .font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                              .padding(.horizontal, 11)
                              .padding(.vertical, 8)
                              .background(
                                  model.selectedTerminalID == terminal.id
                                      ? Color.white.opacity(0.16) : Color.white.opacity(0.06),
                                  in: Capsule()
                              )
                          }
                          .buttonStyle(.plain)
                          .contextMenu {
                              Button("Close terminal", systemImage: "xmark", role: .destructive) {
                                  model.closeTerminal(terminal.id)
                              }
                          }
                      }
                  }
                  .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                  .padding(.vertical, 9)
              }
              .background(Color.white.opacity(0.04))
          }
      
          private func output(_ terminal: RemoteTerminal) -> some View {
              ScrollViewReader { reader in
                  ScrollView([.vertical, .horizontal]) {
                      Text(terminal.visibleOutput.isEmpty ? "Starting login shell…" : terminal.visibleOutput)
                          .font(.system(size: 12, design: .monospaced))
                          .foregroundStyle(terminal.visibleOutput.isEmpty ? .secondary : .primary)
                          .textSelection(.enabled)
                          .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .topLeading)
                          .padding(12)
                          .id("terminal-end")
                  }
                  .defaultScrollAnchor(.bottom)
                  .onChange(of: terminal.output.count) { _, _ in
                      reader.scrollTo("terminal-end", anchor: .bottom)
                  }
              }
          }
      
          private func input(_ terminal: RemoteTerminal) -> some View {
              HStack(spacing: 8) {
                  TextField(terminal.isRunning ? "Type a command" : "Process exited", text: $command)
                      .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                      .autocorrectionDisabled()
                      .font(.system(.body, design: .monospaced))
                      .focused($inputFocused)
                      .submitLabel(.send)
                      .onSubmit { submit(to: terminal.id) }
                      .disabled(!terminal.isRunning)
                  Button { submit(to: terminal.id) } label: {
                      Image(systemName: "arrow.up.circle.fill").font(.title2)
                  }
                  .disabled(!terminal.isRunning || command.isEmpty)
              }
              .padding(10)
              .background(.ultraThinMaterial)
          }
      
          private func keyStrip(_ id: UUID) -> some View {
              ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                  HStack(spacing: 7) {
                      terminalKey("esc") { model.sendTerminalBytes([0x1B], to: id) }
                      terminalKey("tab") { model.sendTerminalBytes([0x09], to: id) }
                      terminalKey("⌃C") { model.sendTerminalBytes([0x03], to: id) }
                      terminalKey("⌃D") { model.sendTerminalBytes([0x04], to: id) }
                      terminalKey("↑") { model.sendTerminalBytes(Array("\u{1B}[A".utf8), to: id) }
                      terminalKey("↓") { model.sendTerminalBytes(Array("\u{1B}[B".utf8), to: id) }
                      terminalKey("←") { model.sendTerminalBytes(Array("\u{1B}[D".utf8), to: id) }
                      terminalKey("→") { model.sendTerminalBytes(Array("\u{1B}[C".utf8), to: id) }
                      terminalKey("return") { model.sendTerminalBytes([0x0D], to: id) }
                  }
                  .padding(.horizontal, 10)
                  .padding(.vertical, 7)
              }
              .background(Color.white.opacity(0.05))
          }
      
          private func terminalKey(_ title: String, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
              Button(title, action: action)
                  .font(.caption.monospaced().weight(.semibold))
                  .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                  .controlSize(.small)
          }
      
          private func submit(to id: UUID) {
              guard !command.isEmpty else { return }
              model.sendTerminalInput(command, to: id)
              command = ""
              inputFocused = true
          }
      }

      MacCast/SessionNotifier.swift:98:    func terminalNeedsAttention(_ message: String, sessionID: UUID) {
              let content = UNMutableNotificationContent()
              content.title = "Terminal needs you"
              content.body = message
              content.sound = .default
              let request = UNNotificationRequest(
                  identifier: "peering-terminal-\(sessionID.uuidString)",
                  content: content,
                  trigger: nil
              )
              Task {
                  do { try await UNUserNotificationCenter.current().add(request) }
                  catch { notifierLog.error("could not post terminal attention: \(String(describing: error), privacy: .public)") }
              }
          }
      
          private func post(_ notification: SessionAwareness.Notification) {
              // A banner about the screen the user is looking at is noise rather than news, and on
              // a frontmost app iOS would not present it anyway — the check here is what keeps the
              // "not posted" decision in one readable place instead of in the delegate's rules.
              guard isAuthorized, UIApplication.shared.applicationState != .active else { return }
      
              let content = UNMutableNotificationContent()
              content.title = notification.title
              content.body = notification.body
              // Sound for the two that can arrive with the phone in a pocket. "Connected" is
              // confirmation of something the user just did, to someone already holding the phone.
              content.sound = notification.kind == .connected ? nil : .default
      
              let request = UNNotificationRequest(
                  identifier: notification.identifier,
                  content: content,
                  // Nothing is scheduled here. Every one of these is about something that has just
                  // happened, so a trigger would only be a delay.
                  trigger: nil
              )
      
              Task {
                  do {
                      try await UNUserNotificationCenter.current().add(request)
                  } catch {
                      notifierLog.error("could not post \(notification.kind.rawValue, privacy: .public): \(String(describing: error), privacy: .public)")
                  }
              }
          }
      
          /// Takes banners back by the identifier their kind owns. Both lists, because a request
          /// that has been added but not yet delivered sits in the pending one — and it would then
          /// arrive at exactly the wrong moment.
          private func retract(_ kinds: [SessionAwareness.Kind]) {
              guard !kinds.isEmpty else { return }
              let identifiers = kinds.map(\.identifier)
              let center = UNUserNotificationCenter.current()
              center.removeDeliveredNotifications(withIdentifiers: identifiers)
              center.removePendingNotificationRequests(withIdentifiers: identifiers)
          }
      }

      +    case truncatedHeader(have: Int, need: Int)
      +    case invalidSampleCount(Int)
      +    case invalidChannelCount(Int)
      +    case lengthMismatch(declared: Int, actual: Int)
      +}
      +
      +/// One chunk of PCM audio: a fixed header carrying its shape and presentation timestamp,
      +/// followed by interleaved IEEE 754 Float32 samples, little-endian.
      +///
      +/// Binary format because audio arrives at ~100 frames/second on a 20ms chunk budget and JSON
      +/// would spend more bytes on punctuation than on the samples.
      +public struct EncodedAudioFrame: Sendable, Equatable {
      +    public let sampleCount: Int
      +    public let channelCount: Int
      +    public let presentationTime: VideoTimestamp
      +    /// Interleaved Float32 samples, in sample-frame order (L0 R0 L1 R1 …).
      +    public let samples: Data
      +
      +    public init(
      +        sampleCount: Int,
      +        channelCount: Int,
      +        presentationTime: VideoTimestamp,
      +        samples: Data
      +    ) {
      +        self.sampleCount = sampleCount
      +        self.channelCount = channelCount
      +        self.presentationTime = presentationTime
      +        self.samples = samples
      +    }
      +
      +    /// sampleCount(4) + channelCount(4) + ptsValue(8) + ptsTimescale(4)
      +    public static let headerSize = 20
      +
      +    public func encoded() -> Data {
      +        var out = Data(capacity: Self.headerSize + samples.count)
      +        out.appendBigEndian(UInt32(clamping: sampleCount))
      +        out.appendBigEndian(UInt32(clamping: channelCount))
      +        out.appendBigEndian(UInt64(bitPattern: presentationTime.value))
      +        out.appendBigEndian(UInt32(bitPattern: presentationTime.timescale))
      +        out.append(samples)
      +        return out
      +    }
      +
      +    public init(decoding payload: Data) throws {
      +        guard payload.count >= Self.headerSize else {
      +            throw AudioWireError.truncatedHeader(have: payload.count, need: Self.headerSize)
      +        }
      +        let base = payload.startIndex
      +        let sampleCount = Int(payload.readBigEndianUInt32(at: base))
      +        let channelCount = Int(payload.readBigEndianUInt32(at: base + 4))
      +        let ptsValue = payload.readBigEndianUInt64(at: base + 8)
      +        let ptsTimescale = payload.readBigEndianUInt32(at: base + 16)
      +        let body = Data(payload[(base + Self.headerSize)...])
      +
      +        guard sampleCount > 0 else {
      +            throw AudioWireError.invalidSampleCount(sampleCount)
      +        }
      +        // Core Audio channel counts are small. Besides rejecting nonsense, this bound keeps
      +        // malformed frames from driving an unbounded channel loop in the renderer.
      +        guard (1...32).contains(channelCount) else {
      +            throw AudioWireError.invalidChannelCount(channelCount)
      +        }
      +        let (sampleValues, sampleOverflow) = sampleCount.multipliedReportingOverflow(by: channelCount)
      +        let (declaredByteCount, byteOverflow) = sampleValues.multipliedReportingOverflow(by: MemoryLayout<Float>.size)
      +        guard !sampleOverflow, !byteOverflow, declaredByteCount == body.count else {
      +            throw AudioWireError.lengthMismatch(
      +                declared: sampleOverflow || byteOverflow ? .max : declaredByteCount,
      +                actual: body.count
      +            )
      +        }
      +
      +        self.init(
      +            sampleCount: sampleCount,
      +            channelCount: channelCount,
      +            presentationTime: VideoTimestamp(
      +                value: Int64(bitPattern: ptsValue),
      +                timescale: Int32(bitPattern: ptsTimescale)
      +            ),
      +            samples: body
      +        )
      +    }
      +}

## Assistant

The keep-awake fix is now verified against macOS itself. The rebuilt host reports both active assertions:

- `NoDisplaySleepAssertion` — keeps the open display awake.
- `PreventSystemSleep` — keeps the Mac/system session awake.

So the switch is no longer cosmetic. There is correctly no permission prompt; macOS exposes this through power assertions, not a protected permission. The new host build is running now.

      import Darwin
      import Foundation
      import RemoteCore
      import os
      
      private let terminalLog = Logger(subsystem: "pl.dmq.peering.host", category: "terminal")
      
      /// Owns login shells independently of network connections. A disconnected phone can list the
      /// sessions later and receives the bounded output history before live output resumes.
      final class TerminalSessionManager: @unchecked Sendable {
          typealias Sink = @Sendable (TerminalEvent) -> Void
      
          private final class Session: @unchecked Sendable {
              let id: UUID
              let ownerClientID: String
              let name: String
              let process: Process
              let master: FileHandle
              var isRunning = true
              var history = Data()
              var promptTail = ""
              var lastAttention: (kind: TerminalAttentionKind, time: Date)?
      
              init(id: UUID, ownerClientID: String, name: String, process: Process, master: FileHandle) {
                  self.id = id
                  self.ownerClientID = ownerClientID
                  self.name = name
                  self.process = process
                  self.master = master
              }
      
              var info: TerminalSessionInfo {
                  TerminalSessionInfo(id: id, name: name, isRunning: isRunning)
              }
          }
      
          private let lock = NSLock()
          private var sessions: [UUID: Session] = [:]
          private var sinks: [String: Sink] = [:]
          private static let maximumHistoryBytes = 512 * 1024
      
          deinit {
              let active = lock.withLock { Array(sessions.values) }
              for session in active where session.isRunning { session.process.terminate() }
          }
      
          func handle(clientID: String, request: TerminalRequest, sink: @escaping Sink) {
              guard !clientID.isEmpty else {
                  sink(.failed(sessionID: nil, message: "The authenticated device has no identity."))
                  return
              }
              lock.withLock { sinks[clientID] = sink }
      
              switch request {
              case .list:
                  let snapshot = lock.withLock {
                      sessions.values.filter { $0.ownerClientID == clientID }.map(\.info).sorted { $0.name < $1.name }
                  }
                  sink(.snapshot(snapshot))
                  for info in snapshot {
                      let history = lock.withLock {
                          sessions[info.id]?.ownerClientID == clientID ? sessions[info.id]?.history ?? Data() : Data()
                      }
                      if !history.isEmpty { sink(.output(sessionID: info.id, data: history)) }
                  }
      
              case .create(let requestedName):
                  create(ownerClientID: clientID, name: requestedName, sink: sink)
      
              case .input(let id, let data):
                  guard data.count <= 64 * 1024 else {
                      sink(.failed(sessionID: id, message: "Terminal input was too large."))
                      return
                  }
                  guard let session = lock.withLock({ sessions[id] }),
                        session.ownerClientID == clientID, session.isRunning else {
                      sink(.failed(sessionID: id, message: "That terminal is no longer running."))
                      return
                  }
                  do { try session.master.write(contentsOf: data) }
                  catch { sink(.failed(sessionID: id, message: "Could not write to the terminal.")) }
      
              case .resize(let id, let columns, let rows):
                  guard columns > 0, rows > 0,
                        let session = lock.withLock({ sessions[id] }),
                        session.ownerClientID == clientID else { return }
                  var size = winsize(ws_row: rows, ws_col: columns, ws_xpixel: 0, ws_ypixel: 0)
                  if ioctl(session.master.fileDescriptor, TIOCSWINSZ, &size) == -1 {
                      terminalLog.error("could not resize terminal (id.uuidString, privacy: .public)")
                  }
      
              case .close(let id):
                  close(id: id, ownerClientID: clientID)
              }
          }
      
          func disconnect(clientID: String) {
              _ = lock.withLock { sinks.removeValue(forKey: clientID) }
          }
      
          private func create(ownerClientID: String, name requestedName: String, sink: @escaping Sink) {
              let name = normalizedName(requestedName)
              let id = UUID()
              var masterFD: Int32 = -1
              var slaveFD: Int32 = -1
              var size = winsize(ws_row: 30, ws_col: 100, ws_xpixel: 0, ws_ypixel: 0)
              guard openpty(&masterFD, &slaveFD, nil, nil, &size) == 0 else {
                  sink(.failed(sessionID: nil, message: "Could not create a terminal."))
                  return
              }
      
              let master = FileHandle(fileDescriptor: masterFD, closeOnDealloc: true)
              let slave = FileHandle(fileDescriptor: slaveFD, closeOnDealloc: true)
              let process = Process()
              // Foundation's Process can attach the slave descriptors but does not make them the
              // child's controlling terminal. `script` performs the required forkpty/setsid/TIOCSCTTY
              // setup, so job control and password prompts behave like a real Terminal window.
              let shell = loginShell()
              process.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/script")
              process.arguments = ["-q", "/dev/null", shell, "-l"]
              process.currentDirectoryURL = FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser
              var environment = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment
              environment["TERM"] = "xterm-256color"
              environment["COLORTERM"] = "truecolor"
              process.environment = environment
              process.standardInput = slave
              process.standardOutput = slave
              process.standardError = slave
      
              let session = Session(id: id, ownerClientID: ownerClientID, name: name, process: process, master: master)
              process.terminationHandler = { [weak self, weak session] process in
                  guard let self, let session else { return }
                  self.didExit(session, status: process.terminationStatus)
              }
              master.readabilityHandler = { [weak self, weak session] handle in
                  guard let self, let session else { return }
                  let data = handle.availableData
                  if data.isEmpty { handle.readabilityHandler = nil; return }
                  self.received(data, from: session)
              }
      
              do {
                  lock.withLock { sessions[id] = session }
                  try process.run()
                  // The spawned process owns duplicated slave descriptors. Keeping the parent's copy
                  // open would prevent EOF on the master after the shell exits.
                  try? slave.close()
                  sink(.created(session.info))
                  terminalLog.notice("started terminal (name, privacy: .public) (id.uuidString, privacy: .public)")
              } catch {
              Macro(id: "ctrl-l",    title: "⌃L",     keyCode: 37, modifiers: .control),
              Macro(id: "ctrl-a",    title: "⌃A",     keyCode: 0,  modifiers: .control),
              Macro(id: "ctrl-e",    title: "⌃E",     keyCode: 14, modifiers: .control),
              Macro(id: "ctrl-r",    title: "⌃R",     keyCode: 15, modifiers: .control),
          ]
      }
      
      struct RemoteTerminal: Identifiable, Equatable {
          let id: UUID
          var name: String
          var isRunning: Bool
          var output = Data()
          var attention: TerminalAttentionKind?
      
          var visibleOutput: String {
              TerminalTextSanitizer.render(output)
          }
      }

              case relay = "RELAY"
              case direct = "DIRECT"

          private func send(_ event: TerminalEvent, to clientID: String) {
              lock.withLock { sinks[clientID] }?(event)
          }
      
          private func normalizedName(_ requested: String) -> String {
              let trimmed = requested.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
              let safe = String(trimmed.filter { character in
                  !character.isNewline && !character.unicodeScalars.contains {
                      CharacterSet.controlCharacters.contains($0)
                  }
              }.prefix(40))
              if !safe.isEmpty { return safe }
              return "Terminal \(lock.withLock { sessions.count + 1 })"
          }
      
          private func loginShell() -> String {
              if let record = getpwuid(getuid()), let shell = record.pointee.pw_shell {
                  let path = String(cString: shell)
                  if !path.isEmpty { return path }
              }
              return ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["SHELL"] ?? "/bin/zsh"
          }
      
          private static func detectAttention(in rawText: String) -> TerminalAttentionKind? {
              let plain = rawText.replacingOccurrences(
                  of: "\\u{001B}\\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]",
                  with: "",
                  options: .regularExpression
              ).lowercased()
              let tail = String(plain.suffix(400))
              if tail.range(of: #"(?:password|passphrase)(?: for [^:\n]+)?:\s*$"#, options: .regularExpression) != nil {
                  return .password
              }
              let confirmationPatterns = [
                  #"(?:\[|\()(?:y/n|yes/no)(?:\]|\))\??\s*$"#,
                  #"are you sure[^\n]*\??\s*$"#,
                  #"press (?:enter|return)[^\n]*$"#,
                  #"continue\?\s*$"#,
              ]
              return confirmationPatterns.contains {
                  tail.range(of: $0, options: .regularExpression) != nil
              } ? .confirmation : nil
          }
      }
